  827:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $liveMirror.Reason + " - an unread mirror" +
  828:             " head is never a matching one")
  829:         exit 1
  830:     }
  831:     if ($liveMirror.Sha -ne $MirrorHead) {
  832:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror head changed since construction" +
  833:             " (recorded " + $MirrorHead + ", live " + $liveMirror.Sha +
  834:             ") - something wrote into the mirror")
  835:         exit 1
  836:     }
  837: 
  838:     $liveStatus = Get-StatusSha256 $RepoRoot
  839:     if (-not $liveStatus.Ok) {
  840:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status could not be captured (" +
  841:             $liveStatus.Reason + ") - an unmade measurement is never a clean" +
  842:             " one")
  843:         exit 1
  844:     }
  845:     if ($liveStatus.Sha -ne $SourceStatusSha256) {
  846:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status changed since construction" +
  847:             " - a tracked, untracked or ignored review input moved without" +
  848:             " moving either head, so the mirror no longer carries what the" +
  849:             " source holds. Rebuild the mirror.")
  850:         exit 1
  851:     }
  852: 
  853:     # THE MIRROR-SIDE FINGERPRINT, recomputed live. Catches an
  854:     # uncommitted edit to a file the mirror's own HEAD still calls clean,
  855:     # or a file added to the mirror after construction - the case the
  856:     # two-HEAD gate cannot see because neither head moves for it.
  857:     $liveMirrorState = Get-StatusSha256 $MirrorPath
  858:     if (-not $liveMirrorState.Ok) {
  859:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror's current state could not be" +
  860:             " measured (" + $liveMirrorState.Reason + ") - an unmade" +
  861:             " measurement is never a clean one")
  862:         exit 1
  863:     }
  864:     if ($liveMirrorState.Sha -ne $MirrorStateSha256) {
  865:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror's contents changed since" +
  866:             " construction - something edited or added a file inside the" +
  867:             " mirror without moving its head. Rebuild the mirror.")
  868:         exit 1
  869:     }
  870: 
  871:     Write-Output "identity: verified"
  872:     exit 0
  873: }
  874: 
  875: # RESOLVE ONCE, THROUGH THE PROVIDER, BEFORE ANY GUARD. An earlier
  876: # revision guarded `[IO.Path]::GetFullPath($MirrorPath)`, which resolves a
  877: # relative path against the PROCESS working directory, while `Test-Path`
  878: # and `Remove-Item -Recurse -Force` further down resolve the same parameter
  879: # against PowerShell's PROVIDER location. When a caller's two locations
  880: # differ - any in-session use after Set-Location - the guard approved one
  881: # absolute target and the recursive delete destroyed another. Found by the
  882: # mode-diff review, 2026-07-28; a prior review had called it theoretical,
  883: # which was wrong: -MirrorPath is a public parameter and the divergence is
  884: # an ordinary PowerShell condition. From here down, only the resolved
  885: # values are used.
  886: $MirrorPath = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($MirrorPath)
  887: if ($OverrideOut) {
  888:     $OverrideOut = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($OverrideOut)
  889: }
  890: 
  891: # -EXTRAINPUT, PARSED BY HAND out of the remaining-argument catch-all.
  892: # PowerShell's own binder refuses to bind one named parameter twice, even
  893: # to an array, so a literal repeatable `-ExtraInput <path>` cannot be a
  894: # declared parameter. Anything left in $BuildRemainingArgs that is not an
  895: # "-ExtraInput <value>" pair is a parameter this tool does not have -
  896: # this is also what refuses a re-mint or reseal flag: there is
  897: # deliberately no such mode, so any flag claiming to be one lands here
  898: # and is rejected the same way an unrecognized parameter always is.
  899: $ExtraInputPaths = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
  900: # Measured on both hosts: when NOTHING would otherwise land in a
  901: # ValueFromRemainingArguments array, -File invocation still hands back
  902: # one PHANTOM $null element instead of a genuinely empty array. Neither
  903: # an -ExtraInput flag name nor a real value is ever $null or empty, so
  904: # filtering both out removes exactly the phantom and nothing a real
  905: # caller could have meant.
  906: $remaining = @($BuildRemainingArgs | Where-Object { $_ })
  907: $ri = 0
  908: while ($ri -lt $remaining.Count) {
  909:     $tok = [string]$remaining[$ri]
  910:     if ($tok -cne "-ExtraInput") {
  911:         Write-Output ("ERROR: unrecognized parameter '" + $tok + "'")
  912:         exit 2
  913:     }
  914:     if ($ri + 1 -ge $remaining.Count) {
  915:         Write-Output "ERROR: -ExtraInput requires a value"
  916:         exit 2
  917:     }
  918:     $eiPath = [string]$remaining[$ri + 1]
  919:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $eiPath -PathType Leaf)) {
  920:         Write-Output ("ERROR: -ExtraInput '" + $eiPath + "' is not a file")
  921:         exit 2
  922:     }
  923:     [void]$ExtraInputPaths.Add((Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $eiPath).Path)
  924:     $ri += 2
  925: }
  926: 
  927: # OVERLAP GUARD, before anything is created or deleted. -Force recursively
  928: # deletes MirrorPath, so a MirrorPath equal to, inside, or containing
  929: # RepoRoot would destroy the user's working tree. robocopy over an
  930: # overlapping pair is equally unsafe. This runs FIRST, because by the time
  931: # Remove-Item runs it is too late to check.
  932: $rr = $RepoRoot.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
  933: $mp = $MirrorPath.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
  934: $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
  935: if ($mp.Equals($rr, $cmp)) {
  936:     Write-Output "ERROR: the mirror path is the repo root itself"
  937:     exit 2
  938: }
  939: if ($mp.StartsWith($rr, $cmp)) {
  940:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the mirror path is inside the repo ($MirrorPath)" +
  941:         " - building or forcing there would write into, or delete, the" +
  942:         " tree under review")
  943:     exit 2
  944: }
  945: if ($rr.StartsWith($mp, $cmp)) {
  946:     Write-Output ("ERROR: the mirror path contains the repo ($MirrorPath)" +
  947:         " - -Force would delete the repo with it")
  948:     exit 2
  949: }
  950: 
  951: # Resolve the EFFECTIVE override path here, default included, and guard it
  952: # beside the mirror guard. Deferring the default until after the mirror is
  953: # built, copied, remediated and manifested would mean discovering a stale
  954: # or overlapping artifact only after all that work had already happened,
  955: # and -SkipProbe would bypass the check entirely.
  956: if (-not $OverrideOut) {
  957:     $OverrideOut = Join-Path (Split-Path $MirrorPath -Parent) `
  958:         ((Split-Path $MirrorPath -Leaf) + ".skills-override.txt")
  959: }
  960: $op = $OverrideOut.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
  961: foreach ($protected in @($rr, $mp)) {
  962:     if (($op + "/").Equals($protected, $cmp) -or
  963:         ($op + "/").StartsWith($protected, $cmp) -or
  964:         $protected.StartsWith($op + "/", $cmp)) {
  965:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the override path overlaps a protected tree" +
  966:             " ($OverrideOut)")
  967:         exit 2
  968:     }
  969: }
  970: if (Test-Path $OverrideOut) {
  971:     Write-Output ("ERROR: $OverrideOut already exists - a stale override" +
  972:         " reads exactly like a fresh one")
  973:     exit 2
  974: }
  975: 
  976: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
  977: # PATH BUDGET PRE-FLIGHT. Runs here, after the overlap and override
  978: # guards and BEFORE anything is created or deleted, because the failure
  979: # it prevents is a MID-COPY one: robocopy stops at the first destination
  980: # it cannot create, leaving a partially populated mirror that reads
  981: # exactly like a complete one.
  982: #
  983: # THE UNIVERSE, frozen text: every file and directory destination that
  984: # the exact `robocopy /E` operation may create beneath the resolved
  985: # mirror root, including tracked, untracked, ignored, and all `.git`
  986: # content. Two consequences that narrower readings get wrong - a
  987: # directory with no files in it is still a destination, and `.git` is
  988: # copied so `.git` counts.
  989: #
  990: # THE LIMIT is 260 characters as a conservative policy across both
  991: # supported PowerShell hosts. It is a deterministic refusal threshold,
  992: # not a claim about the maximum any host, API, OS configuration, or
  993: # downstream client could support.
  994: #
  995: # THE ARITHMETIC is resolved mirror-root length, plus separator, plus
  996: # the relative destination path length.
  997: $PathBudget = 260
  998: $budgetRoot = $MirrorPath.TrimEnd("\", "/")
  999: $budgetRootLen = $budgetRoot.Length
 1000: 
 1001: # -OverrideOut is written BESIDE the mirror by this script, not by
 1002: # robocopy, so the copy universe never covers it. Its own check or none.
 1003: if ($OverrideOut.Length -ge $PathBudget) {
 1004:     Write-Output ("ERROR: path budget exceeded by the override file - " +
 1005:         "$OverrideOut is $($OverrideOut.Length) characters and the limit " +
 1006:         "is $PathBudget")
 1007:     exit 2
 1008: }
 1009: 
 1010: if ($budgetRootLen -ge $PathBudget) {
 1011:     Write-Output ("ERROR: path budget exceeded by the mirror root alone - " +
 1012:         "root is $budgetRootLen characters and the limit is $PathBudget")
 1013:     exit 2
 1014: }
 1015: 
 1016: $srcRoot = $RepoRoot.TrimEnd("\", "/")
 1017: # The prefix stripped to form a relative path, rebuilt rather than
 1018: # derived from a length. A drive root trims to "C:", and "C:".Length + 1
 1019: # is 3 only by coincidence of that one case; on any root ending in a
 1020: # colon the arithmetic silently under-measures every relative path,
 1021: # which is the permissive direction.
 1022: $srcPrefix = $srcRoot
 1023: if (-not $srcPrefix.EndsWith("\")) { $srcPrefix = $srcPrefix + "\" }
 1024: 
 1025: # The ROOT is a source path too. Checking only entries below it leaves
 1026: # the one reparse point most likely to exist unmeasured, and the
 1027: # contract clause says "a source reparse point" with no exemption.
 1028: try {
 1029:     $rootAttr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($srcRoot)
 1030: } catch {
 1031:     Write-Output ("ERROR: $srcRoot could not be enumerated, so the path " +
 1032:         "budget was never measured: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 1033:     exit 2
 1034: }
 1035: if (($rootAttr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) {
 1036:     Write-Output ("ERROR: $srcRoot is a reparse point - the mirror refuses " +
 1037:         "to measure or copy across one")
 1038:     exit 2
 1039: }
 1040: 
 1041: $deepestLen = -1
 1042: $deepestRel = ""
 1043: $dirAttr = [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory
 1044: $reparseAttr = [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint
 1045: 
 1046: # Every directory link target this walk has already followed. Case
 1047: # insensitive because the filesystem is.
 1048: $followedTargets = New-Object "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" (
 1049:     [System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)
 1050: 
 1051: # Every directory link the walk meets that is NOT itself behind another
 1052: # link: its relative path under the source root and its resolved
 1053: # absolute target. The copy runs with /XJD and creates nothing beneath a
 1054: # link, so a link is ONE destination and the entries beneath it are not
 1055: # measured against the budget. The walk still DESCENDS through it,
 1056: # because the cycle refusal and the overlap refusal apply to every link
 1057: # the manifest can reach, and a link behind a link is reachable; a walk
 1058: # that stopped at the outer link never saw an inner cycle (cross-vendor
 1059: # round 1, 2026-09-05). A link behind a link is not recorded: it exists
 1060: # in the target and is reached through the outer junction.
 1061: $sourceLinks = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
 1062: 
 1063: $pending = New-Object System.Collections.Stack
 1064: $pending.Push(@{ Path = $srcRoot; UnderLink = $false })
 1065: while ($pending.Count -gt 0) {
 1066:     $item = $pending.Pop()
 1067:     $dir = $item.Path
 1068:     $underLink = $item.UnderLink
 1069:     $entries = $null
 1070:     try {
 1071:         $entries = @([System.IO.Directory]::GetFileSystemEntries($dir))
 1072:     } catch {
 1073:         # An unreadable path BLOCKS. It is never skipped: a path that
 1074:         # cannot be measured is not a path known to fit, and the same
 1075:         # hole semantics the manifest builder states apply here.
 1076:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $dir could not be enumerated, so the path " +
 1077:             "budget was never measured: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 1078:         exit 2
 1079:     }
 1080:     foreach ($entry in $entries) {
 1081:         $attr = 0
 1082:         try {
 1083:             $attr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($entry)
 1084:         } catch {
 1085:             Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry could not be enumerated, so the " +
 1086:                 "path budget was never measured: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 1087:             exit 2
 1088:         }
 1089:         # A source directory reparse point is NOT copied through: the
 1090:         # copy runs with /XJD, which excludes directory junctions and
 1091:         # directory symbolic links, so the link is one destination and
 1092:         # nothing beneath it is. The re-link step after the final sweep
 1093:         # re-creates it as a junction. The walk still enters it, for the
 1094:         # two refusals below only: measured 2026-09-05, the manifest
 1095:         # expansion starts a listing at every link it finds, so a link
 1096:         # onto one of its own ancestors or a tree whose links reach one
 1097:         # target twice must be refused wherever it sits.
 1098:         $isDirLink = $false
 1099:         if (($attr -band $reparseAttr) -ne 0 -and ($attr -band $dirAttr) -ne 0) {
 1100:             $isDirLink = $true
 1101:             # git's optional index refresh writes the repository's own
 1102:             # .git/index during every status capture. That is a write
 1103:             # into the repository and never through a link only while
 1104:             # .git is not itself a link, so one is refused here rather
 1105:             # than assumed away (cross-vendor round 2, 2026-09-05).
 1106:             if ([System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($entry) -ieq ".git") {
 1107:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry - .git is a directory link, and" +
 1108:                     " the status captures write the repository's own index," +
 1109:                     " so this tree cannot be measured without writing" +
 1110:                     " through a link")
 1111:                 exit 2
 1112:             }
 1113:             $target = $null
 1114:             try {
 1115:                 $item = Get-Item -LiteralPath $entry -Force -ErrorAction Stop
 1116:                 $target = $item.Target
 1117:                 # Windows PowerShell 5.1 hands back a string COLLECTION here
 1118:                 # and PowerShell 7 hands back a plain string, so take the
 1119:                 # first element of anything that is not already a string
 1120:                 # rather than testing for one collection type.
 1121:                 if ($null -ne $target -and $target -isnot [string]) {
 1122:                     foreach ($candidate in $target) { $target = $candidate; break }
 1123:                 }
 1124:             } catch {
 1125:                 $target = $null
 1126:             }
 1127:             if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($target)) {
 1128:                 # An unresolvable link is the same class as an unreadable
 1129:                 # directory: a path that cannot be measured is not a path
 1130:                 # known to fit.
 1131:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry is a directory reparse point " +
 1132:                     "whose target could not be read, so the path budget was " +
 1133:                     "never measured")
 1134:                 exit 2
 1135:             }
 1136: 
 1137:             $entryFull = ""
 1138:             $targetFull = ""
 1139:             try {
 1140:                 $entryFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($entry)
 1141:                 # A RELATIVE TARGET IS RELATIVE TO THE LINK, NOT TO US.
 1142:                 # `GetFullPath` with one argument resolves against the
 1143:                 # PROCESS working directory, so a relative symbolic link
 1144:                 # target resolved to wherever this script happened to be
 1145:                 # invoked from: two distinct targets could compare equal
 1146:                 # and read as a repeat, and a genuine self-cycle could
 1147:                 # compare against the wrong absolute path and be missed.
 1148:                 # Junctions always store an absolute target, which is why
 1149:                 # the junction tests did not show it; symbolic links do
 1150:                 # not. Combine-then-normalize rather than the two-argument
 1151:                 # overload, which .NET Framework 4.8 does not carry.
 1152:                 if ([System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($target)) {
 1153:                     $targetFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($target)
 1154:                 } else {
 1155:                     $linkParent = [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($entryFull)
 1156:                     $targetFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath(
 1157:                         [System.IO.Path]::Combine($linkParent, $target))
 1158:                 }
 1159:             } catch {
 1160:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry is a directory reparse point " +
 1161:                     "whose target " + $target + " is not a usable path, so " +
 1162:                     "the path budget was never measured")
 1163:                 exit 2
 1164:             }
 1165:             $targetPrefix = $targetFull.TrimEnd("\") + "\"
 1166:             if ($entryFull.Equals($targetFull, "OrdinalIgnoreCase") -or
 1167:                 $entryFull.StartsWith($targetPrefix, "OrdinalIgnoreCase")) {
 1168:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry points at $targetFull, which " +
 1169:                     "contains it - following that link would never " +
 1170:                     "terminate, so the mirror refuses it")
 1171:                 exit 2
 1172:             }
 1173:             if (-not $followedTargets.Add($targetFull)) {
 1174:                 # Two links onto one target is not itself a cycle, but it is
 1175:                 # indistinguishable from one here without walking the whole
 1176:                 # graph, and refusing is the direction that cannot
 1177:                 # mismeasure.
 1178:                 Write-Output ("ERROR: $entry points at $targetFull, which " +
 1179:                     "another link in this tree already reaches - the mirror " +
 1180:                     "refuses a tree whose links overlap")
 1181:                 exit 2
 1182:             }
 1183:             if (-not $underLink) {
 1184:                 [void]$sourceLinks.Add(@{ Rel = $entry.Substring($srcPrefix.Length)
 1185:                                           Target = $targetFull })
 1186:             }
 1187:         }
 1188:         # Only a destination counts. The link itself is one; nothing
 1189:         # beneath any link is created by the copy.
 1190:         if (-not $underLink) {
 1191:             $rel = $entry.Substring($srcPrefix.Length)
 1192:             if ($rel.Length -gt $deepestLen) {
 1193:                 $deepestLen = $rel.Length
 1194:                 $deepestRel = $rel
 1195:             }
 1196:         }
 1197:         if (($attr -band $dirAttr) -ne 0) {
 1198:             $pending.Push(@{ Path = $entry; UnderLink = ($underLink -or $isDirLink) })
 1199:         }
 1200:     }
 1201: }
 1202: 
 1203: if ($deepestLen -ge 0) {
 1204:     $deepestSum = $budgetRootLen + 1 + $deepestLen
 1205:     if ($deepestSum -ge $PathBudget) {
 1206:         Write-Output ("ERROR: path budget exceeded - mirror root is " +
 1207:             "$budgetRootLen characters, the deepest relative destination " +
 1208:             "is $deepestLen characters, their sum is $deepestSum, and the " +
 1209:             "limit is $PathBudget. Build the mirror at a shorter path. " +
 1210:             "Deepest: $deepestRel")
 1211:         exit 2
 1212:     }
 1213: }
 1214: 
 1215: # LINK TARGETS ARE PROTECTED TREES. The overlap guard above compares the
 1216: # mirror and override paths against the source root only, and a link
 1217: # target lives outside it. Cross-vendor round 1 (2026-09-05) named the
 1218: # case: -MirrorPath at a link's target with -Force would delete the
 1219: # user's reference checkout, and -OverrideOut inside it would write
 1220: # there. Round 2 added two more: an INNER link's target is reached by
 1221: # the manifest just the same, so every target the walk followed is
 1222: # protected, not only the recorded outer links; and a path spelled
 1223: # THROUGH some other link can land inside a protected target without
 1224: # matching its spelling, so a mirror or override path with a reparse
 1225: # point among its existing ancestors is refused outright rather than
 1226: # compared. This runs before anything is created or deleted.
 1227: function Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink($path) {
 1228:     # The path itself, then each existing ancestor up to the drive root:
 1229:     # is any of them a reparse point? Attributes are read directly
 1230:     # rather than after a Test-Path, because a DANGLING junction is a
 1231:     # reparse point Test-Path may report as absent; a missing entry is
 1232:     # the one condition that skips a level, and it is recognised by the
 1233:     # exception type, never by a false from a helper. Returns the
 1234:     # offending path or $null.
 1235:     $probe = ([string]$path).TrimEnd("\", "/")
 1236:     while ($probe -and -not [string]::IsNullOrEmpty([System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($probe))) {
 1237:         $pa = 0
 1238:         $missing = $false
 1239:         try {
 1240:             $pa = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($probe)
 1241:         } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
 1242:             $missing = $true
 1243:         } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
 1244:             $missing = $true
 1245:         } catch {
 1246:             Write-Output ("ERROR: " + $probe + " could not be read while" +
 1247:                 " checking for a directory link: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 1248:             exit 2
 1249:         }
 1250:         if (-not $missing -and (($pa -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0)) {
 1251:             return $probe
 1252:         }
 1253:         $probe = [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($probe)
 1254:     }
 1255:     return $null
 1256: }
 1257: 
 1258: foreach ($pair in @(@("mirror path", $MirrorPath), @("override path", $OverrideOut))) {
 1259:     $label = $pair[0]
 1260:     $hit = Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink $pair[1]
 1261:     if ($hit) {
 1262:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the " + $label + " passes through a directory" +
 1263:             " link at " + $hit + " - a path reached through a link can alias" +
 1264:             " a tree the mirror only links to, so the mirror refuses it")
 1265:         exit 2
 1266:     }
 1267: }
 1268: # A followed TARGET that is itself a link, or sits beneath one, is the
 1269: # alias in the other direction (cross-vendor round 3): the walk records
 1270: # the target as spelled, so a mirror path at the real directory behind
 1271: # it neither passes through a link nor overlaps the recorded text.
 1272: foreach ($target in @($followedTargets)) {
 1273:     $hit = Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink $target
 1274:     if ($hit) {
 1275:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the source link target " + $target + " is" +
 1276:             " itself a directory link or sits beneath one (" + $hit + ") -" +
 1277:             " the mirror cannot protect a tree it cannot name, so it" +
 1278:             " refuses to build")
 1279:         exit 2
 1280:     }
 1281: }
 1282: foreach ($target in @($followedTargets)) {
 1283:     $tp = ([string]$target).Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
 1284:     foreach ($pair in @(@("mirror path", $mp), @("override path", ($op + "/")))) {
 1285:         $label = $pair[0]
 1286:         $cand = $pair[1]
 1287:         if ($cand.Equals($tp, $cmp) -or $cand.StartsWith($tp, $cmp) -or
 1288:             $tp.StartsWith($cand, $cmp)) {
 1289:             Write-Output ("ERROR: the " + $label + " overlaps the target of a" +
 1290:                 " source link (" + $target + ") - building there would write" +
 1291:                 " into, or delete, a tree the mirror only links to")
 1292:             exit 2
 1293:         }
 1294:     }
 1295: }
 1296: 
 1297: if (Test-Path $MirrorPath) {
 1298:     if (-not $Force) {
 1299:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $MirrorPath already exists - a stale mirror" +
 1300:             " reads exactly like a fresh one. Pass -Force to replace it.")
 1301:         exit 2
 1302:     }
 1303:     Remove-Item -LiteralPath $MirrorPath -Recurse -Force
 1304: }
 1305: New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $MirrorPath | Out-Null
 1306: $MirrorPath = (Resolve-Path $MirrorPath).Path
 1307: 
 1308: # CONSTRUCTION BRIDGE, step 1: capture the source identity BEFORE the
 1309: # copy. Everything after this compares against this value, not against a
 1310: # fresh read, because a fresh read cannot tell a stable source from one
 1311: # that moved and moved back.
 1312: $sourceBefore = Get-HeadSha $RepoRoot
 1313: if (-not $sourceBefore.Ok) {
 1314:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $sourceBefore.Reason + " - the mirror's" +
 1315:         " identity in the debate record would be blank on the source side")
 1316:     exit 1
 1317: }
 1318: 
 1319: # The source status, captured BEFORE the copy. Taken here and again
 1320: # after, and required equal, for the same reason as the head: a
 1321: # fingerprint taken only afterwards bakes in any edit that landed during
 1322: # the copy, and every later verify then passes over a mirror carrying
 1323: # older bytes. That direction is fail-OPEN, which this gate may not be.
 1324: $statusBefore = Get-StatusSha256 $RepoRoot
 1325: if (-not $statusBefore.Ok) {
 1326:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status capture failed (" +
 1327:         $statusBefore.Reason + "), so post-construction drift in an" +
 1328:         " untracked or ignored review input would be undetectable")
 1329:     exit 1
 1330: }
 1331: 
 1332: # /XJD: directory junctions and directory symbolic links are not copied
 1333: # through. Measured 2026-09-05: the link is absent from the destination
 1334: # and every ordinary file copies. File links are NOT excluded, so a file
 1335: # symbolic link still lands as its target's bytes, exactly as before.
 1336: & robocopy $RepoRoot $MirrorPath /E /XJD /COPY:DAT /R:1 /W:1 /NFL /NDL /NJH /NJS /NP | Out-Null
 1337: if ($LASTEXITCODE -ge 8) {
 1338:     Write-Output "ERROR: robocopy failed with $LASTEXITCODE"
 1339:     exit 2
 1340: }
 1341: 
 1342: # THE THREE TEST SEAMS, and the rule they must satisfy LITERALLY. Two of
 1343: # them are declared here; the third, $seamFailLinkTarget, is declared at
 1344: # the top of the execution section because Get-FilesBeneath reads it
 1345: # during the source status capture ABOVE, and it only ever ADDS the
 1346: # link-target resolution failure inside that expansion.
 1347: #
 1348: # Each is a BOOLEAN that ORs one extra block condition into a comparison
 1349: # below. None can supply a value, so none can suppress a real
 1350: # measurement, and setting one can only ever ADD a reason to fail.
 1351: # That makes the one-way property structural rather than a property of
 1352: # whatever value someone happens to pass.
 1353: #
 1354: # TWO earlier shapes failed that bar, and both were caught by review
 1355: # rather than by reasoning, which is why the property is now asserted by
 1356: # a test instead of argued in a comment:
 1357: #
 1358: #   1. A copy-source override aimed at a same-HEAD tree with different
 1359: #      ignored content would have BUILT, carrying the wrong non-HEAD
 1360: #      content under a record attesting the real source. Its partner
 1361: #      committed into $RepoRoot, contradicting this tool's promise three
 1362: #      lines from the top that it never writes to the real tree.
 1363: #   2. The replacement INJECTED a value. A parent that set it to the
 1364: #      repo's own current HEAD - trivially readable in advance -
 1365: #      suppressed the genuine post-copy measurement and turned a build
 1366: #      that should have failed into one that passed. That is the exact
 1367: #      inverse of the claim the comment was making.
 1368: #
 1369: # Any parent process can set these variables, so the shape has to be
 1370: # safe on its own, not safe because no shipped caller sets it.
 1371: $seamFailSourceStable = [bool]$env:PARALLAX_MIRROR_SEAM_FAIL_SOURCE_STABLE
 1372: $seamFailCopiedHead = [bool]$env:PARALLAX_MIRROR_SEAM_FAIL_COPIED_HEAD
 1373: 
 1374: $sourceAfter = Get-HeadSha $RepoRoot
 1375: 
 1376: # BRIDGE STEP 3: the source must not have moved while we copied it. A
 1377: # source that moved mid-copy produces a tree that matches no commit.
 1378: if (-not $sourceAfter.Ok) {
 1379:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $sourceAfter.Reason + " after the copy")
 1380:     exit 1
 1381: }
 1382: if ($seamFailSourceStable -or ($sourceAfter.Sha -ne $sourceBefore.Sha)) {
 1383:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source head moved during construction (" +
 1384:         $sourceBefore.Sha + " to " + $sourceAfter.Sha + ") - the copied tree" +
 1385:         " matches neither commit")
 1386:     exit 1
 1387: }
 1388: 
 1389: # BRIDGE STEP 4: the COPIED tree must carry the source's head. Without
 1390: # this the record can hold two individually valid SHAs while nothing
 1391: # proves the mirror was built from the recorded source commit - two true
 1392: # facts arranged to look like one.
 1393: $copiedHead = Get-HeadSha $MirrorPath
 1394: if (-not $copiedHead.Ok) {
 1395:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $copiedHead.Reason + " - the copied tree's" +
 1396:         " identity could not be measured")
 1397:     exit 1
 1398: }
 1399: if ($seamFailCopiedHead -or ($copiedHead.Sha -ne $sourceBefore.Sha)) {
 1400:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror was not built from this source (source" +
 1401:         " head " + $sourceBefore.Sha + ", copied head " + $copiedHead.Sha + ")")
 1402:     exit 1
 1403: }
 1404: 
 1405: # The second half of the status bridge: re-capture and require equal, so
 1406: # an edit that landed during the copy fails the build instead of being
 1407: # baked into the record. Never re-derived from the mirror, which is about
 1408: # to be remediated and would then disagree by design.
 1409: $sourceStatus = Get-StatusSha256 $RepoRoot
 1410: if (-not $sourceStatus.Ok) {
 1411:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status capture failed (" +
 1412:         $sourceStatus.Reason + "), so post-construction drift in an" +
 1413:         " untracked or ignored review input would be undetectable")
 1414:     exit 1
 1415: }
 1416: if ($sourceStatus.Sha -ne $statusBefore.Sha) {
 1417:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: a source review input changed during" +
 1418:         " construction, so the mirror carries neither the before nor the" +
 1419:         " after state of the tree")
 1420:     exit 1
 1421: }
 1422: 
 1423: $found = Get-BackChannelEntry $MirrorPath
 1424: if (-not $found.Ok) {
 1425:     Write-Output ("ERROR: could not enumerate back-channels in the" +
 1426:         " mirror: " + $found.Reason)
 1427:     exit 2
 1428: }
 1429: $entries = $found.Entries
 1430: $tracked = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
 1431: foreach ($entry in $entries) {
 1432:     if (Test-Tracked $MirrorPath $entry) { [void]$tracked.Add($entry) }
 1433:     $full = Join-Path $MirrorPath $entry
 1434:     if (Test-Path $full) { Remove-Item -LiteralPath $full -Recurse -Force }
 1435:     # Prune the directories the entry lived in, up to but never including
 1436:     # the mirror root. `git ls-files` names FILES, so deleting one leaves
 1437:     # `.agents/skills/<name>/` standing - an empty shell of the very
 1438:     # surface this step exists to remove.
 1439:     $dir = Split-Path (Join-Path $MirrorPath $entry) -Parent
 1440:     while ($dir -and ($dir.Length -gt $MirrorPath.Length) -and
 1441:            (Test-Path $dir) -and
 1442:            -not (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $dir -Force)) {
 1443:         Remove-Item -LiteralPath $dir -Force
 1444:         $dir = Split-Path $dir -Parent
 1445:     }
 1446: }
 1447: if ($tracked.Count -gt 0) {
 1448:     # Suppress repository hooks for the remediation commit BELOW. The
 1449:     # mirror carries the real repo's .git, hooks included (see the
 1450:     # BLOCKED text on the commit call), so committing here without
 1451:     # redirecting core.hooksPath would run whatever hook the reviewed
 1452:     # repo ships. The directory lives inside the MIRROR's own .git,
 1453:     # where a plain filesystem copy carries it along for free but
 1454:     # `git status`/`ls-files` never see it, so it cannot register as a
 1455:     # back-channel or dirty the porcelain itself.
 1456:     #
 1457:     # Created fresh and VERIFIED empty in this same run, then used for
 1458:     # BOTH git calls below. Never fall back to committing with hooks
 1459:     # live: either check failing exits BLOCKED before either call runs.
 1460:     $hooksDir = Join-Path $MirrorPath (Join-Path ".git" "parallax-mirror-hooks")
 1461:     try {
 1462:         New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $hooksDir -Force -ErrorAction Stop | Out-Null
 1463:     } catch {
 1464:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: could not create an empty hooks directory" +
 1465:             " for the remediation commit (" + $_.Exception.Message + ")." +
 1466:             " Never fall back to committing with hooks live.")
 1467:         exit 1
 1468:     }
 1469:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $hooksDir -PathType Container)) {
 1470:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the hooks directory for the remediation" +
 1471:             " commit is not a directory. Never fall back to committing" +
 1472:             " with hooks live.")
 1473:         exit 1
 1474:     }
 1475:     $hooksDirEntries = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $hooksDir -Force)
 1476:     if ($hooksDirEntries.Count -gt 0) {
 1477:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the hooks directory for the remediation" +
 1478:             " commit is not empty (" + $hooksDirEntries.Count +
 1479:             " entries). Never fall back to committing with hooks live.")
 1480:         exit 1
 1481:     }
 1482:     $hooksOverride = "core.hooksPath=" + $hooksDir
 1483: 
 1484:     # Stage exactly the entries that were tracked. `git add -A` over a
 1485:     # pathspec would fail when one of the two patterns matches nothing,
 1486:     # and `git add -A` over the whole tree would stage the untracked and
 1487:     # ignored files the mirror deliberately carries.
 1488:     $paths = @($tracked)
 1489:     & git -C $MirrorPath -c $hooksOverride add -- @paths 2>&1 | Out-Null
 1490:     if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
 1491:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: could not stage the removed back-channels" +
 1492:             " in the mirror")
 1493:         exit 1
 1494:     }
 1495:     & git -C $MirrorPath -c $hooksOverride -c user.email=parallax@local `
 1496:         -c user.name=parallax commit -q -m "remove instruction back-channels for review" 2>&1 | Out-Null
 1497:     if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) {
 1498:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror commit failed. The mirror carries" +
 1499:             " the real repo's .git, hooks included, so this is a" +
 1500:             " mirror-construction problem, never a finding about the" +
 1501:             " reviewed work.")
 1502:         exit 1
 1503:     }
 1504: }
 1505: 
 1506: $after = Get-BackChannelEntry $MirrorPath
 1507: if (-not $after.Ok) {
 1508:     Write-Output ("ERROR: could not re-enumerate back-channels in the" +
 1509:         " mirror: " + $after.Reason)
 1510:     exit 2
 1511: }
 1512: if ($after.Entries.Count -gt 0) {
 1513:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: back-channel(s) survived remediation: " +
 1514:         ($after.Entries -join "; "))
 1515:     exit 1
 1516: }
 1517: 
 1518: # COPY EACH -ExtraInput IN NOW, as part of construction and BEFORE the
 1519: # baseline and the manifest are taken below, so the identity record
 1520: # covers it and the printed record enumerates it - it lands as an
 1521: # ordinary untracked mirror file, the same as any other review input the
 1522: # mirror cannot inherit on its own.
 1523: # Two refusals BEFORE any of them is copied, so a rejected build never
 1524: # leaves half the inputs in place. Every input is flattened to its leaf
 1525: # name, because the mirror re-roots every path; that makes two different
 1526: # files with one leaf name indistinguishable at the destination, and it
 1527: # lets an outside file land on a name the copied tree already carries.
 1528: # Cross-vendor round 2 found both: the loop wrote with -Force, so the
 1529: # second of a colliding pair silently won and a tracked file could be
 1530: # replaced by unreviewed content that the digest then certified. The
 1531: # plan promises to copy in EACH named file, and there is no second
 1532: # location it ever froze, so the honest answer is to refuse.
 1533: $eiSeen = @{}
 1534: foreach ($ei in @($ExtraInputPaths)) {
 1535:     $leaf = Split-Path $ei -Leaf
 1536:     $key = $leaf.ToLowerInvariant()
 1537:     if ($eiSeen.ContainsKey($key)) {
 1538:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: two -ExtraInput files share the destination" +
 1539:             " name '" + $leaf + "' (" + $eiSeen[$key] + " and " + $ei +
 1540:             "); the mirror flattens every input to its leaf name, so one" +
 1541:             " would silently replace the other")
 1542:         exit 1
 1543:     }
 1544:     $eiSeen[$key] = $ei
 1545:     $dest = Join-Path $MirrorPath $leaf
 1546:     if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dest) {
 1547:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: -ExtraInput '" + $leaf + "' would overwrite" +
 1548:             " a file the mirror already has (" + $dest + "); reviewed" +
 1549:             " content must not be replaced by unreviewed content the" +
 1550:             " digest would then certify")
 1551:         exit 1
 1552:     }
 1553: }
 1554: foreach ($ei in @($ExtraInputPaths)) {
 1555:     $dest = Join-Path $MirrorPath (Split-Path $ei -Leaf)
 1556:     Copy-Item -LiteralPath $ei -Destination $dest -Force
 1557: }
 1558: # THE LAST SWEEP, after every writer into the mirror. The enumeration
 1559: # above proves REMEDIATION worked. This one proves nothing put a
 1560: # back-channel back afterwards. SKILL.md calls the enumeration the
 1561: # PRIMARY control for the reviewed tree's skills and agents, "not
 1562: # defence in depth", and a control that runs before the last writer
 1563: # into the tree it clears is not that control. -ExtraInput is the only
 1564: # shipped writer below the sweep above; this sweep sits after ALL of
 1565: # them so the next writer added inherits it rather than slipping under
 1566: # it. Measured 2026-08-31: without this, an AGENTS.md named as an
 1567: # -ExtraInput reached the finished mirror and only the client probe
 1568: # refused it.
 1569: $final = Get-BackChannelEntry $MirrorPath
 1570: if (-not $final.Ok) {
 1571:     Write-Output ("ERROR: could not re-enumerate back-channels in the" +
 1572:         " finished mirror: " + $final.Reason)
 1573:     exit 2
 1574: }
 1575: if ($final.Entries.Count -gt 0) {
 1576:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: back-channel(s) present in the finished" +
 1577:         " mirror: " + ($final.Entries -join "; ") + ". Remediation" +
 1578:         " cleared the mirror, so this was carried back in afterwards -" +
 1579:         " an -ExtraInput may not name an instruction channel.")
 1580:     exit 1
 1581: }
 1582: 
 1583: # RE-LINK, after the last writer and the last sweep. Remediation deletes
 1584: # what it finds with Remove-Item, and after this step that path would
 1585: # reach THROUGH a junction into the user's real reference checkout. So
 1586: # the junctions are created here, once nothing above can delete again,
 1587: # and the check that follows is READ-ONLY.
 1588: #
 1589: # Always a JUNCTION, whatever the source had. Measured 2026-09-05: a
 1590: # symbolic link needs a privilege this tool cannot assume and mklink /D
 1591: # refused it; New-Item -ItemType Junction succeeded on both hosts with
 1592: # none. The reviewer reads a junction as a directory.
 1593: foreach ($lnk in @($sourceLinks)) {
 1594:     $dest = Join-Path $MirrorPath $lnk.Rel
 1595:     if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dest) {
 1596:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the copy created '" + $lnk.Rel + "' although" +
 1597:             " /XJD excludes directory links, so the mirror already holds" +
 1598:             " something at a path that must be a junction")
 1599:         exit 1
 1600:     }
 1601:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $lnk.Target -PathType Container)) {
 1602:         Write-Output ("ERROR: the link target " + $lnk.Target + " for '" +
 1603:             $lnk.Rel + "' could not be read at re-link time, so the mirror" +
 1604:             " would be missing a review input that reads as complete")
 1605:         exit 2
 1606:     }
 1607:     $parent = Split-Path $dest -Parent
 1608:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $parent -PathType Container)) {
 1609:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the parent directory of '" + $lnk.Rel + "'" +
 1610:             " is missing from the mirror, so the copy did not produce the" +
 1611:             " tree the walk measured")
 1612:         exit 1
 1613:     }
 1614:     try {
 1615:         New-Item -ItemType Junction -Path $dest -Target $lnk.Target `
 1616:             -ErrorAction Stop | Out-Null
 1617:     } catch {
 1618:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: could not re-create '" + $lnk.Rel + "' as a" +
 1619:             " junction onto " + $lnk.Target + ": " + $_.Exception.Message)
 1620:         exit 1
 1621:     }
 1622:     # Read the junction back. A link that does not resolve to the
 1623:     # recorded target is a mirror pointing somewhere the record does not
 1624:     # say, which is the case the whole identity gate exists to refuse.
 1625:     $made = $null
 1626:     try {
 1627:         $made = (Get-Item -LiteralPath $dest -Force -ErrorAction Stop).Target
 1628:         if ($null -ne $made -and $made -isnot [string]) {
 1629:             foreach ($candidate in $made) { $made = $candidate; break }
 1630:         }
 1631:     } catch { $made = $null }
 1632:     if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($made)) {

 1634:             " created but its target could not be read back")
 1635:         exit 1
 1636:     }
 1637:     $madeFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($made).TrimEnd("\")
 1638:     if (-not $madeFull.Equals($lnk.Target.TrimEnd("\"), "OrdinalIgnoreCase")) {
 1639:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the junction at '" + $lnk.Rel + "' resolves" +
 1640:             " to " + $madeFull + " rather than the recorded " + $lnk.Target)
 1641:         exit 1
 1642:     }
 1643: }
 1644: 
 1645: # THE LINK SWEEP, read-only by construction. git walks a junction onto a
 1646: # plain folder file by file, so a back-channel behind a link is now
 1647: # enumerable, and deleting it would delete from the user's real tree.
 1648: # Any such entry is a BLOCK with its path named; nothing here removes
 1649: # anything. A link onto a directory holding its own .git is ONE entry to
 1650: # git and is not descended; that blind spot is backlog item 92, and it
 1651: # is the same blind spot the copy had.
 1652: if (@($sourceLinks).Count -gt 0) {
 1653:     $linkSweep = Get-BackChannelEntry $MirrorPath
 1654:     if (-not $linkSweep.Ok) {
 1655:         Write-Output ("ERROR: could not enumerate back-channels behind the" +
 1656:             " re-linked paths: " + $linkSweep.Reason)
 1657:         exit 2
 1658:     }
 1659:     $behind = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
 1660:     foreach ($e in @($linkSweep.Entries)) {

 1664:             if ($eNorm.StartsWith($prefix, "OrdinalIgnoreCase")) {
 1665:                 [void]$behind.Add($eNorm)
 1666:                 break
 1667:             }
 1668:         }
 1669:     }
 1670:     if ($behind.Count -gt 0) {

 1672:             ($behind -join "; ") + ". The build never deletes through a" +
 1673:             " link; remove the file from the link's target and rebuild.")
 1674:         exit 1
 1675:     }

1215: # LINK TARGETS ARE PROTECTED TREES. The overlap guard above compares the
1216: # mirror and override paths against the source root only, and a link
1217: # target lives outside it. Cross-vendor round 1 (2026-09-05) named the
1218: # case: -MirrorPath at a link's target with -Force would delete the
1219: # user's reference checkout, and -OverrideOut inside it would write
1220: # there. Round 2 added two more: an INNER link's target is reached by
1221: # the manifest just the same, so every target the walk followed is
1222: # protected, not only the recorded outer links; and a path spelled
1223: # THROUGH some other link can land inside a protected target without
1224: # matching its spelling, so a mirror or override path with a reparse
1225: # point among its existing ancestors is refused outright rather than
1226: # compared. This runs before anything is created or deleted.
1227: function Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink($path) {
1228:     # The path itself, then each existing ancestor up to the drive root:
1229:     # is any of them a reparse point? Attributes are read directly
1230:     # rather than after a Test-Path, because a DANGLING junction is a
1231:     # reparse point Test-Path may report as absent; a missing entry is
1232:     # the one condition that skips a level, and it is recognised by the
1233:     # exception type, never by a false from a helper. Returns the
1234:     # offending path or $null.
1235:     $probe = ([string]$path).TrimEnd("\", "/")
1236:     while ($probe -and -not [string]::IsNullOrEmpty([System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($probe))) {
1237:         $pa = 0
1238:         $missing = $false
1239:         try {
1240:             $pa = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($probe)
1241:         } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
1242:             $missing = $true
1243:         } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
1244:             $missing = $true
1245:         } catch {
1246:             Write-Output ("ERROR: " + $probe + " could not be read while" +
1247:                 " checking for a directory link: " + $_.Exception.Message)
1248:             exit 2
1249:         }
1250:         if (-not $missing -and (($pa -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0)) {
1251:             return $probe
1252:         }
1253:         $probe = [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($probe)
1254:     }
1255:     return $null
1256: }

1348: # Each is a BOOLEAN that ORs one extra block condition into a comparison
1349: # below. None can supply a value, so none can suppress a real
1350: # measurement, and setting one can only ever ADD a reason to fail.
1351: # That makes the one-way property structural rather than a property of
1352: # whatever value someone happens to pass.
1353: #
1354: # TWO earlier shapes failed that bar, and both were caught by review
1355: # rather than by reasoning, which is why the property is now asserted by
1356: # a test instead of argued in a comment:
1357: #
1358: #   1. A copy-source override aimed at a same-HEAD tree with different
1359: #      ignored content would have BUILT, carrying the wrong non-HEAD
1360: #      content under a record attesting the real source. Its partner
1361: #      committed into $RepoRoot, contradicting this tool's promise three
1362: #      lines from the top that it never writes to the real tree.
1363: #   2. The replacement INJECTED a value. A parent that set it to the
1364: #      repo's own current HEAD - trivially readable in advance -
1365: #      suppressed the genuine post-copy measurement and turned a build
1366: #      that should have failed into one that passed. That is the exact
1367: #      inverse of the claim the comment was making.
1368: #
1369: # Any parent process can set these variables, so the shape has to be
1370: # safe on its own, not safe because no shipped caller sets it.
1371: $seamFailSourceStable = [bool]$env:PARALLAX_MIRROR_SEAM_FAIL_SOURCE_STABLE
1372: $seamFailCopiedHead = [bool]$env:PARALLAX_MIRROR_SEAM_FAIL_COPIED_HEAD
1373: 
1374: $sourceAfter = Get-HeadSha $RepoRoot
1375: 
1376: # BRIDGE STEP 3: the source must not have moved while we copied it. A
1377: # source that moved mid-copy produces a tree that matches no commit.
1378: if (-not $sourceAfter.Ok) {
1379:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $sourceAfter.Reason + " after the copy")
1380:     exit 1
1381: }
1382: if ($seamFailSourceStable -or ($sourceAfter.Sha -ne $sourceBefore.Sha)) {
1383:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source head moved during construction (" +
1384:         $sourceBefore.Sha + " to " + $sourceAfter.Sha + ") - the copied tree" +
1385:         " matches neither commit")
1386:     exit 1
1387: }
1388: 
1389: # BRIDGE STEP 4: the COPIED tree must carry the source's head. Without
1390: # this the record can hold two individually valid SHAs while nothing
1391: # proves the mirror was built from the recorded source commit - two true
1392: # facts arranged to look like one.
1393: $copiedHead = Get-HeadSha $MirrorPath
1394: if (-not $copiedHead.Ok) {
1395:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $copiedHead.Reason + " - the copied tree's" +
1396:         " identity could not be measured")
1397:     exit 1
1398: }
1399: if ($seamFailCopiedHead -or ($copiedHead.Sha -ne $sourceBefore.Sha)) {
1400:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror was not built from this source (source" +
1401:         " head " + $sourceBefore.Sha + ", copied head " + $copiedHead.Sha + ")")
1402:     exit 1
1403: }
1404: 
1405: # The second half of the status bridge: re-capture and require equal, so
1406: # an edit that landed during the copy fails the build instead of being
1407: # baked into the record. Never re-derived from the mirror, which is about
1408: # to be remediated and would then disagree by design.
1409: $sourceStatus = Get-StatusSha256 $RepoRoot
1410: if (-not $sourceStatus.Ok) {
1411:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source status capture failed (" +
1412:         $sourceStatus.Reason + "), so post-construction drift in an" +
1413:         " untracked or ignored review input would be undetectable")
1414:     exit 1
1415: }
1416: if ($sourceStatus.Sha -ne $statusBefore.Sha) {
1417:     Write-Output ("BLOCKED: a source review input changed during" +
1418:         " construction, so the mirror carries neither the before nor the" +
1419:         " after state of the tree")
1420:     exit 1
1421: }

322: function Format-StatusPathname($path) {
323:     # Reproduce the porcelain LINE form's quoting for one pathname, so a
324:     # recorded baseline is byte-comparable with a capture taken by running
325:     # THE STATUS COMMAND directly - which is what
326:     # references/backup-lane.md requires of every round.
327:     #
328:     # The rule is one condition because the guard has already removed every
329:     # other trigger. Measured 2026-07-29: `git status --porcelain` quotes
330:     # each pathname independently and, once `"` , `\` and the control
331:     # characters are refused, a SPACE is the only remaining trigger. In the
332:     # same measurement a rename with a spaced source and a plain
333:     # destination printed `R  "with space/a.lua" -> plain/a.lua`, and the
334:     # reverse printed `R  plain/b.lua -> "with space/b.lua"` - each side on
335:     # its own.
336:     #
337:     # Non-ASCII is deliberately NOT quoted. The old captures set
338:     # `core.quotepath=false`, so an accented pathname was recorded raw, and
339:     # this render must keep that shape rather than introduce quoting the
340:     # baseline never had.
341:     if ([string]$path -match ' ') { return ('"' + $path + '"') }
342:     return [string]$path
343: }

345: function Format-StatusRecord($record) {
346:     # Render one record in git's own DISPLAY order, `R  <old> -> <new>`, so
347:     # the recorded baseline keeps the shape references/backup-lane.md
348:     # already describes and two captures stay comparable across this
349:     # change.
350:     #
351:     # ONE WAY ONLY. Nothing re-parses this text: the manifest's subjects
352:     # come from the RECORDS. That separation is the whole reason the arrow
353:     # is safe to write here at all, and Test-SupportedPathname refusing `>`
354:     # is what keeps it unambiguous.
355:     $head = [string]$record.X + [string]$record.Y + " "
356:     if ($record.Source) {
357:         return ($head + (Format-StatusPathname $record.Source) + " -> " +
358:                 (Format-StatusPathname $record.Path))
359:     }
360:     return ($head + (Format-StatusPathname $record.Path))
361: }

363: function Invoke-GitFields($repo, $gitArgs) {
364:     # Every git capture that produces PATHNAMES goes through here, so both
365:     # of them answer the same questions the same way.
366:     #
367:     # `core.quotepath=false` is GONE from these captures and its absence is
368:     # deliberate. The flag governed the DISPLAY form, and `-z` has no
369:     # display form: measured 2026-07-29, `git ls-files -z` returned raw
370:     # UTF-8 bytes with the flag ABSENT. Keeping a flag that does nothing
371:     # invites a later reader to treat it as load-bearing.
372:     #
373:     # The console-encoding guard the old capture needed is gone for the
374:     # same reason: nothing here goes through PowerShell's decoder. The
375:     # bytes are read from the process stream and decoded once, strictly,
376:     # in ConvertFrom-NulCapture.
377:     $captured = Invoke-GitProcess $repo $gitArgs
378:     if (-not $captured.Ok) {
379:         return @{ Ok = $false; Fields = @(); Reason = $captured.Reason }
380:     }
381:     return (ConvertFrom-NulCapture $captured.Bytes)
382: }

384: function Get-BackChannelEntry($repo) {
385:     # One listing covering tracked, untracked AND ignored files. `--others`
386:     # without `--exclude-standard` includes ignored paths. `*AGENTS.md`
387:     # reaches any depth; `.agents/*` is anchored at the repo ROOT and does
388:     # NOT, which is the asymmetry recorded in SKILL.md's
389:     # enumeration-depth-asymmetry region. Do not restate "at any depth"
390:     # here.
391:     #
392:     # Returns @{Ok=..; Entries=..; Reason=..}. A function returning a bare
393:     # @() has its empty array unrolled by PowerShell, so the caller's
394:     # variable becomes $null and a CLEAN repo reads exactly like a FAILED
395:     # enumeration.
396:     $r = Invoke-GitFields $repo @("ls-files", "--cached", "--others", "-z",
397:                                   "*AGENTS.md", ".agents/*", '.kimi-code/*')
398:     if (-not $r.Ok) {
399:         return @{ Ok = $false; Entries = @(); Reason = $r.Reason }
400:     }
401:     # An entry this script cannot handle exactly is a stop, on either of
402:     # the guard's two grounds - a name whose resolution would risk the
403:     # WRONG file, or a name it could resolve but could not record.
404:     # Reporting either as clean is the one outcome the whole preflight
405:     # exists to prevent, and a back-channel is the case where that matters
406:     # most.
407:     foreach ($e in @($r.Fields)) {
408:         if (-not (Test-SupportedPathname $e)) {
409:             return @{ Ok = $false; Entries = @()
410:                       Reason = ("the back-channel entry '" + $e + "' names" +
411:                         " a path this script cannot handle exactly, so it" +
412:                         " cannot be enumerated, deleted and recorded as a" +
413:                         " single consistent fact") }
414:         }
415:     }
416:     return @{ Ok = $true; Entries = @($r.Fields) }
417: }

419: function Test-Tracked($repo, $path) {
420:     & git -C $repo ls-files --error-unmatch -- $path 2>$null | Out-Null
421:     return ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0)
422: }

424: function Get-BaselineRaw($repo) {
425:     # THE STATUS COMMAND, every capture without exception. Bare porcelain
426:     # OMITS ignored paths and COLLAPSES an untracked directory to one
427:     # entry; ignored content is the entire reason this workspace is a
428:     # mirror.
429:     #
430:     # The BASELINE is this raw capture, status codes included
431:     # (references/backup-lane.md). It is recorded verbatim and is NOT the
432:     # same object as the manifest's subject list below.
433:     #
434:     # Same structured shape and same reason as Get-BackChannelEntry: an
435:     # empty baseline is a legitimate state, and a bare @() return would be
436:     # indistinguishable from a failed capture.
437:     return (Invoke-GitFields $repo @("status", "--porcelain", "--ignored",
438:                                      "-uall", "-z"))
439: }

441: function Get-ManifestSubject($records) {
442:     # Coverage is exactly the baseline's paths. Returns @{Paths=..} or
443:     # @{Error=..}.
444:     #
445:     # Two dispositions are DEFINED and are not the same thing as skipping.
446:     # A deletion-only entry is OMITTED, deliberately, because it has no
447:     # bytes; an `RD` destination is a STOP. What must never happen is a
448:     # third thing: an entry passed over because this function could not
449:     # make sense of it. That is the silent hole in the manifest, and it is
450:     # what the stops below exist to prevent.
451:     #
452:     # Takes RECORDS, never rendered text. A rename's destination is already
453:     # the record's Path under `-z`, so there is no arrow to search for and
454:     # no chance of splitting inside a pathname.
455:     $paths = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
456:     foreach ($r in @($records)) {
457:         $x = $r.X
458:         $y = $r.Y
459:         # Deletion-only entries have no bytes to hash. HEAD plus the
460:         # baseline already bind the absence, which is the whole content of
461:         # the fact, so OMIT them.
462:         if (($x -eq " " -and $y -eq "D") -or ($x -eq "D" -and $y -eq " ")) {
463:             continue
464:         }
465:         # An `RD` destination no longer exists. That entry names no
466:         # readable file, so it is a stop rather than a silent omission.
467:         if ($y -eq "D" -and ($x -eq "R" -or $x -eq "C")) {
468:             return @{ Error = ("baseline entry '" +
469:                 (Format-StatusRecord $r) + "' names a destination that has" +
470:                 " been deleted; the mirror is not in a state this manifest" +
471:                 " rule can describe") }
472:         }
473:         [void]$paths.Add($r.Path)
474:     }
475:     return @{ Paths = @($paths) }
476: }

478: function Get-FilesBeneath($start, $visited, $depth) {
479:     # Every file beneath $start, INCLUDING files behind every directory
480:     # link found under it, as full paths. Measured 2026-09-05 on both
481:     # hosts: Get-ChildItem -Recurse enters a link only when the link IS
482:     # the start path; from a parent it lists the link and does not pass
483:     # through it. So each link beneath a subject gets a listing of its
484:     # own, started at the link. $visited holds every resolved link
485:     # target this expansion has entered, seeded by the caller with the
486:     # repository root; a repeat is a refusal. Cross-vendor round 2
487:     # (2026-09-05) showed the visited set alone is not a cycle guard: a
488:     # RELATIVE symbolic-link target resolved against the link's spelled
489:     # parent yields a new string on every level of a cycle reached
490:     # through an outer junction. $depth is the bound that closes that:
491:     # a link nested more than 16 links deep is refused. Returns
492:     # @{Paths=..} or @{Error=..}.
493:     if ($depth -gt 16) {
494:         return @{ Error = ("'" + $start + "' sits more than 16 directory" +
495:             " links deep; the manifest refuses a link graph that deep" +
496:             " because a relative-link cycle presents exactly like it") }
497:     }
498:     $out = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
499:     $startAttr = 0
500:     try {
501:         $startAttr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($start)
502:     } catch {
503:         return @{ Error = ("'" + $start + "' could not be read: " +
504:                            $_.Exception.Message) }
505:     }
506:     if (($startAttr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) {
507:         $target = $null
508:         try {
509:             $target = (Get-Item -LiteralPath $start -Force -ErrorAction Stop).Target
510:             if ($null -ne $target -and $target -isnot [string]) {
511:                 foreach ($candidate in $target) { $target = $candidate; break }
512:             }
513:         } catch { $target = $null }
514:         if ([string]::IsNullOrEmpty($target)) {
515:             return @{ Error = ("'" + $start + "' is a directory link whose" +
516:                                " target could not be read") }
517:         }
518:         $targetFull = ""
519:         # WRAPPED, because this script never sets $ErrorActionPreference
520:         # to Stop: a .NET exception here is NON-TERMINATING, so an
521:         # unwrapped GetFullPath left $targetFull empty, put the empty
522:         # string into the visited set, and returned Paths with no Error -
523:         # a failed measurement reading as a clean one. Measured by the
524:         # cross-vendor reviewer with a 270-character target
525:         # (PathTooLongException), 2026-09-05.
526:         try {
527:             if ([System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($target)) {
528:                 $targetFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($target)
529:             } else {
530:                 $targetFull = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath(
531:                     [System.IO.Path]::Combine(
532:                         [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName(
533:                             [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($start)), $target))
534:             }
535:         } catch {
536:             return @{ Error = ("'" + $start + "' is a directory link whose" +
537:                 " target " + $target + " could not be resolved to a full" +
538:                 " path: " + $_.Exception.Message) }
539:         }
540:         if ($seamFailLinkTarget) {
541:             return @{ Error = ("'" + $start + "' link target resolution" +
542:                 " failed (test seam)") }
543:         }
544:         if (-not $visited.Add($targetFull.TrimEnd("\"))) {
545:             return @{ Error = ("'" + $start + "' reaches " + $targetFull +
546:                 " which this expansion already entered; the manifest refuses" +
547:                 " a link graph that repeats or cycles") }
548:         }
549:     }
550:     $found = $null
551:     try {
552:         $found = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $start -Recurse -File -Force `
553:             -ErrorAction Stop
554:     } catch {
555:         return @{ Error = ("'" + $start + "' could not be enumerated: " +
556:                            $_.Exception.Message) }
557:     }
558:     foreach ($f in $found) { [void]$out.Add($f.FullName) }
559:     $links = $null
560:     try {
561:         $links = @(Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $start -Recurse -Directory -Force `
562:             -ErrorAction Stop | Where-Object {
563:                 ($_.Attributes -band [System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0 })
564:     } catch {
565:         return @{ Error = ("'" + $start + "' could not be enumerated for" +
566:                            " links: " + $_.Exception.Message) }
567:     }
568:     foreach ($l in $links) {
569:         $inner = Get-FilesBeneath $l.FullName $visited ($depth + 1)
570:         if ($inner.ContainsKey("Error")) { return $inner }
571:         foreach ($p in @($inner.Paths)) { [void]$out.Add($p) }
572:     }
573:     return @{ Paths = @($out) }
574: }

576: function Get-ContentManifest($repo, $paths) {
577:     # Directories expand RECURSIVELY to their files: a directory subject
578:     # such as References/ is never one manifest entry, because a hash over
579:     # a directory name identifies nothing.
580:     # A directory link beneath a subject is expanded by a listing started
581:     # at the link (Get-FilesBeneath), because a recursion from the parent
582:     # does not pass through it on either host.
583:     $files = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
584:     foreach ($p in $paths) {
585:         $full = Join-Path $repo $p
586:         if (Test-Path $full -PathType Container) {
587:             # -Stop, never the default: a swallowed enumeration error
588:             # omits every file under an unreadable subdirectory and the
589:             # manifest then reads as coverage of a tree it never saw.
590:             # Seeded with the repository root: a link back onto the root
591:             # is a cycle on its first step, not its second.
592:             $visited = New-Object "System.Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]" (
593:                 [System.StringComparer]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)
594:             [void]$visited.Add([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($repo).TrimEnd("\"))
595:             $beneath = Get-FilesBeneath $full $visited 0
596:             if ($beneath.ContainsKey("Error")) {
597:                 return @{ Error = ("'" + $p + "': " + $beneath.Error) }
598:             }
599:             foreach ($fullName in @($beneath.Paths)) {
600:                 $rel = $fullName.Substring($repo.Length + 1)
601:                 [void]$files.Add($rel.Replace("\", "/"))
602:             }
603:         } elseif (Test-Path $full -PathType Leaf) {
604:             [void]$files.Add($p.TrimEnd("/").Replace("\", "/"))
605:         } else {
606:             # A baseline path with nothing behind it is a stop. Skipping it
607:             # would leave a hole in the manifest that reads as coverage.
608:             return @{ Error = "baseline path '$p' has no file behind it" }
609:         }
610:     }
611:     $unique = [string[]]@($files | Sort-Object -Unique)
612:     [Array]::Sort($unique, [System.StringComparer]::Ordinal)
613:     $sha = [System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create()
614:     $out = New-Object System.Collections.ArrayList
615:     foreach ($rel in $unique) {
616:         # A failed ReadAllBytes is NON-TERMINATING on both hosts
617:         # (measured 2026-08-04), so without this catch $bytes keeps the
618:         # PREVIOUS file's contents and this line records that hash for
619:         # the file it could not read - deterministically, so the wrong
620:         # value reproduces on every later run. Evidence that quietly
621:         # names the wrong bytes is worse than no evidence.
622:         $bytes = $null
623:         try {
624:             $bytes = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllBytes((Join-Path $repo $rel))
625:         } catch {
626:             $sha.Dispose()
627:             return @{ Error = ("'" + $rel + "' could not be read: " +
628:                                $_.Exception.Message) }
629:         }
630:         $hex = ([System.BitConverter]::ToString($sha.ComputeHash($bytes)) -replace '-', '').ToLower()
631:         [void]$out.Add($rel + " " + $hex)
632:     }
633:     return @{ Paths = @($out) }
634: }

636: function Get-HeadSha($repo) {
637:     # @{Ok=$true; Sha=..} or @{Ok=$false; Reason=..}. Structured for the
638:     # same reason every other capture here is: a bare empty string cannot
639:     # be told apart from a failed read, and an unread HEAD must never
640:     # compare equal to anything.
641:     $out = (& git -C $repo rev-parse HEAD 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
642:     if (($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) -or -not $out) {
643:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = "could not resolve HEAD in $repo" }
644:     }
645:     return @{ Ok = $true; Sha = $out }
646: }
647: 
648: function Get-CombinedSha256($fields, $manifestLines) {
649:     # ONE digest over a raw status capture's fields plus a content
650:     # manifest's lines, NUL-joined - the separator git already emits
651:     # them with, so no field value can forge a boundary. Shared by the
652:     # source-side fingerprint below and the mirror-side one at
653:     # construction and verify time, so the two are computed the same way
654:     # by construction rather than by two call sites staying in sync.
655:     $joined = ((@($fields) + @($manifestLines)) -join "`0")
656:     $bytes = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($joined)
657:     $sha = [System.Security.Cryptography.SHA256]::Create()
658:     try { $digest = $sha.ComputeHash($bytes) } finally { $sha.Dispose() }
659:     return (($digest | ForEach-Object { $_.ToString("x2") }) -join "")
660: }
661: 
662: function Get-StatusSha256($repo) {
663:     # A non-HEAD fingerprint for whichever repo it is given: the SAME
664:     # status capture the mirror already uses, PLUS the content manifest
665:     # of the paths that status names, combined by Get-CombinedSha256.
666:     # Used on the SOURCE for source_status_sha256 and on the MIRROR
667:     # itself for mirror_state_sha256 - one algorithm, so the two
668:     # fingerprints cannot drift apart by drifting the code that computes
669:     # them.
670:     #
671:     # THE CONTENT HALF IS NOT OPTIONAL, and measured 2026-08-04 rather
672:     # than assumed: editing an already-ignored file leaves the status
673:     # listing byte-identical, so a status-only fingerprint verified clean
674:     # across exactly the drift this check exists to catch. Status alone
675:     # sees a path APPEAR or DISAPPEAR or change code; it does not see the
676:     # bytes behind it change, and the bytes are the review input.
677:     $captured = Get-BaselineRaw $repo
678:     if (-not $captured.Ok) {
679:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = $captured.Reason }
680:     }
681:     $parsedStatus = ConvertTo-StatusRecord $captured.Fields
682:     if ($parsedStatus.ContainsKey("Error")) {
683:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = $parsedStatus.Error }
684:     }
685:     $subj = Get-ManifestSubject $parsedStatus.Records
686:     if ($subj.ContainsKey("Error")) {
687:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = $subj.Error }
688:     }
689:     $content = Get-ContentManifest $repo $subj.Paths
690:     if ($content.ContainsKey("Error")) {
691:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = $content.Error }
692:     }
693:     return @{ Ok = $true
694:               Sha = (Get-CombinedSha256 $captured.Fields $content.Paths) }
695: }

711: $RepoRoot = (Resolve-Path $RepoRoot).Path

713: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
714: # VERIFY MODE - step 6 of the construction bridge, run immediately before
715: # every fresh and resumed dispatch.
716: #
717: # WHAT IT PROVES: the two-HEAD gate proves committed-HEAD freshness.
718: # Non-HEAD inputs are bound in the constructed mirror's manifest AT
719: # CONSTRUCTION TIME, and changes after construction are caught by TWO
720: # status-based fingerprints, one per side. The source-status comparison
721: # catches source-side drift WHEN IT IS VISIBLE TO IT: changes that move
722: # the status listing, or that alter the content of a path the listing
723: # names. The mirror-state comparison catches the same class of change
724: # made directly INSIDE the mirror after construction - an uncommitted
725: # edit to a file the mirror's own HEAD still calls clean, or a file
726: # added to the mirror - which the two-HEAD gate cannot see because
727: # neither head moves. A tracked file git reports CLEAN, on either side,
728: # is covered by neither fingerprint, so a raw-byte change surviving the
729: # clean filter unchanged is NOT covered. The contract region carries the
730: # same qualification; this comment said "are detected" flat until round
731: # 3 of the mode-diff debate pointed out that the code and the contract
732: # had drifted apart again, in the direction of the code claiming more.
733: #
734: # Two further refusals guard the comparison itself, both ahead of any
735: # digest work: the source and the mirror must not be the same directory
736: # - otherwise every remaining comparison is trivially satisfied whenever
737: # the two heads already agree, which they do whenever the mirror needed
738: # no remediation commit - and the live -MirrorPath must canonically
739: # match -ExpectedMirrorPath. Both are CALLER ARGUMENTS in the SAME
740: # invocation rather than values read back from the construction record,
741: # so the guarantee is narrow: it catches a verify whose two path
742: # arguments disagree, and it does NOT refuse a mirror rebuilt somewhere
743: # else with both arguments updated to the new path.
744: #
745: # Neither status comparison is decoration. An edit to an untracked or
746: # ignored review input moves NEITHER head, and that content class is
747: # precisely what the mirror exists to carry, so a gate built only on
748: # HEADs would be blind in the middle of the feature.
749: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
750: if ($VerifyIdentity) {
751:     # Resolve THROUGH THE PROVIDER here too. Build does this below and
752:     # verify did not, so a relative -MirrorPath resolved against the

754:     # for git - the same divergence this file's own history block calls
755:     # an ordinary PowerShell condition. Verify only reads, so the
756:     # realistic failure was a spurious block rather than a wrong pass,
757:     # but a tool should not teach one lesson in one mode and forget it
758:     # in the other.

762:     if ($SourceHead -cnotmatch $shaRx) {
763:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the recorded source head is missing or not a" +
764:             " commit id, so nothing was compared")
765:         exit 1
766:     }
767:     if ($MirrorHead -cnotmatch $shaRx) {
768:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the recorded mirror head is missing or not a" +

779:             " malformed, so nothing was compared")
780:         exit 1
781:     }
782: 
783:     # BEFORE ANY DIGEST WORK. A source pointed at itself as the mirror
784:     # would satisfy every remaining comparison trivially whenever the

793:         exit 1
794:     }

797:     # a call whose two path arguments disagree. It does NOT refuse a

800:     if (-not $mpNorm.Equals($expNorm, $cmp)) {
801:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: -MirrorPath ($MirrorPath) does not match" +
802:             " -ExpectedMirrorPath ($ExpectedMirrorPath) in this same call")
803:         exit 1
804:     }
805: 
806:     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $MirrorPath -PathType Container)) {
807:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror at $MirrorPath is gone, so its" +
808:             " identity could not be measured")
809:         exit 1
810:     }

812:     $liveSource = Get-HeadSha $RepoRoot
813:     if (-not $liveSource.Ok) {
814:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $liveSource.Reason + " - an unread source" +
815:             " head is never a matching one")
816:         exit 1
817:     }
818:     if ($liveSource.Sha -ne $SourceHead) {
819:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the source head moved since construction" +
820:             " (recorded " + $SourceHead + ", live " + $liveSource.Sha +
821:             ") - rebuild the mirror")
822:         exit 1
823:     }
824:

826:     if (-not $liveMirror.Ok) {
827:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: " + $liveMirror.Reason + " - an unread mirror" +
828:             " head is never a matching one")
829:         exit 1

832:         Write-Output ("BLOCKED: the mirror head changed since construction" +

861:             " measurement is never a clean one")
862:         exit 1

866:             " construction - something edited or added a file inside the" +

868:         exit 1

871:     Write-Output "identity: verified"
872:     exit 0

662: function Get-StatusSha256($repo) {
663:     # A non-HEAD fingerprint for whichever repo it is given: the SAME
664:     # status capture the mirror already uses, PLUS the content manifest
665:     # of the paths that status names, combined by Get-CombinedSha256.
666:     # Used on the SOURCE for source_status_sha256 and on the MIRROR
667:     # itself for mirror_state_sha256 - one algorithm, so the two
668:     # fingerprints cannot drift apart by drifting the code that computes
669:     # them.
670:     #
671:     # THE CONTENT HALF IS NOT OPTIONAL, and measured 2026-08-04 rather
672:     # than assumed: editing an already-ignored file leaves the status
673:     # listing byte-identical, so a status-only fingerprint verified clean
674:     # across exactly the drift this check exists to catch. Status alone
675:     # sees a path APPEAR or DISAPPEAR or change code; it does not see the
676:     # bytes behind it change, and the bytes are the review input.
677:     $captured = Get-BaselineRaw $repo
678:     if (-not $captured.Ok) {
679:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = $captured.Reason }
680:     }
681:     $parsedStatus = ConvertTo-StatusRecord $captured.Fields
682:     if ($parsedStatus.ContainsKey("Error")) {
683:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = $parsedStatus.Error }
684:     }
685:     $subj = Get-ManifestSubject $parsedStatus.Records
686:     if ($subj.ContainsKey("Error")) {
687:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = $subj.Error }
688:     }
689:     $content = Get-ContentManifest $repo $subj.Paths
690:     if ($content.ContainsKey("Error")) {
691:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = $content.Error }
692:     }
693:     return @{ Ok = $true
694:               Sha = (Get-CombinedSha256 $captured.Fields $content.Paths) }
695: }

718: # Non-HEAD inputs are bound in the constructed mirror's manifest AT

720: # status-based fingerprints, one per side. The source-status comparison
